--- CBR-CDB ---
How many patients in 2100 received central venous catheter placement with guidance after the reopening of recent thoracotomy site procedure within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'central venous catheter placement with guidance' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'central venous catheter placement with guidance'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------

How many hours have passed since the patient 10021487 was admitted to the hospital?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT 24 * (strftime('%J', current_time) - strftime('%J', admissions.admittime)) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10021487 ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.admittime'], 'tab': ['admissions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10021487']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT 24 * ( strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J', admissions.admittime) ) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10021487 AND admissions.dischtime IS NULL
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.admittime'], 'tab': ['admissions'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10021487']]}
----------

Has patient 10039997 received nacl 0.9% in 12/last year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(*)>0 FROM inputevents WHERE inputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10039997 ) ) AND inputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'nacl 0.9%' AND d_items.linksto = 'inputevents' ) AND strftime('%Y',inputevents.starttime) = '2099'
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'inputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10039997'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'nacl 0.9%'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'inputevents'], ["strftime('%y',inputevents.starttime)", '=', '2099']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT(*)>0 FROM inputevents WHERE inputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10039997 ) ) AND inputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'nacl 0.9%' AND d_items.linksto = 'inputevents' ) AND datetime(inputevents.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') AND strftime('%m',inputevents.starttime) = '12'
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'inputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10039997'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'nacl 0.9%'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'inputevents'], ["datetime(inputevents.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ["strftime('%m',inputevents.starttime)", '=', '12']]}

Since 2100, what were the five most frequently requested specimen tests?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM microbiologyevents WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01') GROUP BY microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't1.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM microbiologyevents WHERE strftime('%Y',microbiologyevents.charttime) >= '2100' GROUP BY microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't1.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [["strftime('%y',microbiologyevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What was the first recorded height for patient 10020740 during their first hospital encounter?

PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.charttime', 'chartevents.valuenum', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items', 'icustays'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'height'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents']]}

TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.charttime', 'chartevents.valuenum', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items', 'icustays'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'height (cm)'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents']]}

----------

Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'microbiologyevents.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}

Has there been any organism found in the last blood (toxo) microbiology test patient 10038081 received on their last hospital encounter?
----------
PREDS:

Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'microbiologyevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038081'], ['microbiologyevents.org_name', 'is', 'not null'], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT(*)>0 FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038081 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) AND microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND microbiologyevents.org_name IS NOT NULL AND microbiologyevents.charttime = ( SELECT DISTINCT microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents WHERE microbiologyevents.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038081 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) AND microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc = 'blood (toxo)' AND microbiologyevents.org_name IS NOT NULL ORDER BY microbiologyevents.charttime DESC LIMIT 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'microbiologyevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038081'], ['microbiologyevents.org_name', 'is', 'not null'], ['microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', '=', 'blood (toxo)']]}

List the top five most frequent procedures that patients underwent within the same month after being diagnosed with diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+1 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What was the ciprofloxacin hcl dosage that patient 10019172 had been prescribed in 11/2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT prescriptions.dose_val_rx FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) AND prescriptions.drug = 'ciprofloxacin hcl' AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100-11' AND prescriptions.starttime = ( SELECT DISTINCT prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) AND prescriptions.drug = 'ciprofloxacin hcl' AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100-11' ORDER BY prescriptions.starttime DESC LIMIT 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.dose_val_rx', 'prescriptions.starttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10019172'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'ciprofloxacin hcl'], ["strftime('%y-%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100-11']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT SUM(prescriptions.dose_val_rx) FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10019172 ) AND prescriptions.drug = 'ciprofloxacin hcl' AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100-11'
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.dose_val_rx'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10019172'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'ciprofloxacin hcl'], ["strftime('%y-%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100-11']]}

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.starttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10005817'], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of month','-0 month')"], ['prescriptions.route', '=', 'iv drip']]}

How much does patient 10007058 change in SpO2 second measured on the first ICU visit compared to the first value measured on the first ICU visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT 
    (SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
     FROM chartevents 
     WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN 
         (SELECT icustays.stay_id 
          FROM icustays 
          WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN 
              (SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
               FROM admissions 
               WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058) 
          AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL 
          ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC 
          LIMIT 1) 
     AND chartevents.itemid IN 
         (SELECT d_items.itemid 
          FROM d_items 
          WHERE d_items.abbreviation = 'spo2' 
          AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents') 
     AND chartevents.charttime = 
         (SELECT DISTINCT chartevents.charttime 
          FROM chartevents 
          WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN 
              (SELECT icustays.stay_id 
               FROM icustays 
               WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN 
                   (SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
                    FROM admissions 
                    WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10007058) 
               AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL 
               ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC 
               LIMIT 1) 
          AND chartevents.itemid IN 
              (SELECT d_items.itemid 
               FROM d_items 
               WHERE d_items.abbreviation = 'spo2' 
               AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents') 
          ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC 
          LIMIT 1 OFFSET 1)) 
    - 
    (SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
     FROM chartevents 
     WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN 
         (SELECT icustays.stay_id 
          FROM icustays 
          WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN 
              (SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
               FROM admissions

Among patients who were diagnosed with acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous during this year, what are the top five most commonly prescribed medications that followed in the same hospital encounter for patients in their 50s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:

How many patients were prescribed the medicine metronidazole (flagyl) within 2 months after a diagnosis of urinary tract infection, site not specified since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'
    ) 
    AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('now', '-1 year')
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime 
    FROM prescriptions 
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'metronidazole (flagyl)'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'metronidazole (flagyl)'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary tract infection, site not specified' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'metronidazole (flagyl)' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'metronidazole (flagyl)'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}

What is the 6-month survival probability for patients living with a other and unspecified angina pectoris diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified angina pectoris' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

Since 1 year ago, what are the top five most common drugs prescribed in the same month to male patients aged 50s after being diagnosed with unspecified sinusitis (chronic)?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified sinusitis (chronic)' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE patients.gender = 'M' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified sinusitis (chronic)'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------

How many people received a introduction of nutritional substance into upper gi, via natural or artificial opening procedure within the same month after they had been diagnosed with postprocedural pneumothorax since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'postprocedural pneumothorax'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'introduction of nutritional substance into upper gi, via natural or artificial opening'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'postprocedural pneumothorax'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'introduction of nutritional substance into upper gi, via natural or artificial opening'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'postprocedural pneumothorax' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'introduction of nutritional substance into upper gi, via natural or artificial opening' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'postprocedural pneumothorax'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'introduction of nutritional substance into upper gi, via natural or artificial opening'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

What are the three most commonly prescribed drugs for patients who were diagnosed with personal history of tobacco use previously in the same month, since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of tobacco use' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'personal history of tobacco use'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

TRUTH:

How many patients underwent reopening of recent thoracotomy site within the same month after the diagnosis with other acute postoperative pain, since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other acute postoperative pain'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other acute postoperative pain'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other acute postoperative pain' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other acute postoperative pain'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'reopening of recent thoracotomy site'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )

For patients diagnosed with unspecified disorder of kidney and ureter, what were the top three most common microbiology tests that followed in the same hospital encounter since 2100?
----------

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of acetonuria survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------

How many patients died during the same hospital encounter after having been diagnosed in 2100 with varices of other sites?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id 
WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
    SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
    FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
    WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'varices of other sites'
) 
AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' 
AND admissions.discharge_location = 'died'
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'varices of other sites'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'varices of other sites' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND strftime('%Y',T1.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%Y',patients.dod) = '2100' ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.hadm_id', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'varices of other sites'], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y',patients.dod)", '=', '2100'], ['t2.hadm_id', '=', 't3.hadm_id']]}

Get me the top four most frequently tested specimens that patients took in the same month after being diagnosed with pleural effusion in other conditions classified elsewhere.
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to male patients in their 30s during the same month after they were diagnosed with hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia during this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
        AND patients.gender = 'M'
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hyposmolality'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

----------

How many patients received albuterol inhaler in the same month after the confirmation of toxic encephalopathy?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'toxic encephalopathy'
    )
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime 
    FROM prescriptions 
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'albuterol inhaler'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'toxic encephalopathy'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'albuterol inhaler'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'toxic encephalopathy' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'albuterol inhaler' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'toxic encephalopathy'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'albuterol inhaler'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE patients.gender = 'M' AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

----------
TRUTH:

Give a list of the four most frequent medications that patients were prescribed during the same hospital encounter after being diagnosed with personal history of colonic polyps since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of colonic polyps'
        ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'personal history of colonic polyps'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of colonic polyps' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'personal history of colonic polyps'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

Give me the top four most commonly prescribed medications in the same month to the patients aged 50s after they were diagnosed with non-st elevation (nstemi) myocardial infarction throughout this year.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'non-st elevation (nstemi) myocardial infarction'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'non-st elevation (nstemi) myocardial infarction'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'non-st elevation (nstemi) myocardial infarction' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'non-st elevation (nstemi) myocardial infarction'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

    FROM (

) AS T3

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with history of falling?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

How many patients underwent single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass during the same month after the diagnosis with arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg, in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'arthropathy, unspecified, lower leg'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime, 'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id 
WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE long_title = 'facial weakness') 
AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now') 
AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL

What are the top five most frequent medications prescribed within 2 months to the patients in their 50s after they were diagnosed with cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

----------

Following the diagnosis of other abnormal blood chemistry , how many patients developed autistic disorder, current or active state within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other abnormal blood chemistry'
    )
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'autistic disorder, current or active state'
    )
) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month')

What are the three most frequent drugs prescribed to female patients with an age of 20s within the same month after they were diagnosed with unspecified acquired hypothyroidism?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        JOIN patients ON admissions.subject_id = patients.subject_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'
        )
        AND patients.gender = 'f'
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

Among patients who were prescribed with phenytoin infatab, what are the top five most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM prescriptions
    WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN (
        SELECT DISTINCT hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        WHERE drug = 'phenytoin infatab' AND strftime('%Y', starttime) = '2100'
    ) AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'phenytoin infatab' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'phenytoin infatab'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many people died after being diagnosed with posttraumatic stress disorder within 2 months throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified.
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'pleural effusion, not elsewhere classified'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}

TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of septic shock within 2 months during the last year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2099'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.dischtime 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE admissions.discharge_location = 'died' 
    AND strftime('%Y', admissions.dischtime) = '2099'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.dischtime 
AND datetime(T2.dischtime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.dischtime', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.dischtime)", '=', '2099'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2099'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.dischtime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}
----------

How much is a bilirubin, indirect lab test?
----------
PREDS:
None
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': [], 'cond': [['test."nothing"', '=', 'none']]}
----------
TRUTH:

Count the number of patients who were diagnosed with hypospadias but didn't come back to the hospital within 2 months in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hypospadias'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) 
AND admissions.subject_id NOT IN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(admissions.dischtime) AND datetime(admissions.dischtime, '+2 month')
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hypospadias'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hypospadias' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hypospadias' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) = '2100' AND datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hypospadias'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of alcoholic cirrhosis of liver within the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m',admissions.dischtime) ) AND admissions.discharge_location = 'died'
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.discharge_location', '=', 'died'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver'], ["strftime('%y-%m',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m',admissions.dischtime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver'], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t3.dod,'start of month')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}
